Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a enzymes algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 312. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 229/1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to enzymes theory, we decompose the problem into 312 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 229. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
